# Third-Party Notices

This file is GENERATED — do not edit it by hand. Regenerate with:

    node scripts/licensing/gen-notices.mjs

It is produced from the dependency graph that is actually built into the
shipped artifacts: the Go modules linked into `vulos-server`/`vulos-init`, the
npm production closure bundled into the web UI, the pinned libraries vendored
into the OS apps, and (when generated against a built rootfs) the Debian
packages installed into the image.

Vulos distributes these components. The licence text of each one is reproduced
below, as those licences require.

**Source code for the GPL/LGPL-licensed components of the Vulos image** is
covered by the written offer in `WRITTEN-OFFER.md` (shipped in the image at
`/opt/vulos/legal/WRITTEN-OFFER.md`), and by the package manifest in
`SOURCES.manifest`.

## Libraries vendored into the OS apps

These are shipped as pre-built JavaScript bundles under `apps/<app>/vendor/`.
The bundler strips the source comments, so the licence text is carried
alongside in `apps/<app>/vendor/LICENSES/` and reproduced here.

The list is the full install closure of the pinned packages, so it may name a
package whose code the bundler dropped — we would rather over-attribute than
miss a notice we owe.

## Debian base system (installed into the Vulos image)

> This copy of the notices was generated on a host without a built Debian
> rootfs, so the package list is not included. The notices file shipped
> inside a Vulos image is regenerated by `build.sh` with the real package
> manifest of that image.

## Third-party names and logos

Vulos displays the names of third-party applications it can install. Trademarks
are the property of their respective owners; their use here is nominative (to
identify the software) and does not imply endorsement.
